## Decisions (release 5.51)

- Decisions 248, 252 and 255 to 257, as the design has them. Decision 259, the timeline's lines, waits for thread D, and the `**` forms come after that.
- **Judgement calls, for the owner's hand test:**
  - **Only what moves is listed in a timing change's preview.** A tie reworded with its date unchanged, as every tie is when the anchor changes, goes straight in the status log, as listing them would hide the dates that do move.
  - **A row form's preview lists what moves with its copies by their ties, ticked,** as well as the copies. The mock listed only the copies, but decision 257 asks for each task's date change, and 5.50 moved them with no message.
  - **Moved by a template's timing, the log says "Template: Product trial".** A task elsewhere tied to one of them says "Follows …", as that's why it moved.
  - **A row others count from can't start repeating.** Its form says so and changes nothing, rather than ending other rows' ties quietly, as a repeating row has no one date to give. On a task, 5.50 ends them, but a task's form warns as you choose it, and the template's lines are in another window.
  - **A row's tie doesn't take if it would make a circle with a task's own tie.** It stays as it was, and stops following. Rows alone can't make one.
  - **A row with subtasks spans them only if it has no Days, no tie and isn't the anchor,** so giving it Days makes it a row like any other. One already tied stays editable.
  - **A tree tied to something outside stays driven from it when the anchor changes.** If the old anchor's task has a tie of its own, such as to an outage, that tie stays, and the new anchor's task stays tied to the old one, as its own, so moving the outage still moves the whole tree. The mock replaced that tie, which would have cut the tree off with nothing in the preview to say so. In any other tree the old anchor's task takes its new tie, and the new anchor's loses only ties that followed its row.
  - **Taking out a row, or the anchor, places those tied to it by hand from then on.** Its preview says who. Tasks already made stay tied, as their own.
  - **Tie… on the anchor is its Start only,** and a tied row's Start is what's typed over, as the mock has them. Its Due is a line beside it.
  - **Typing the anchor's Due with Days moves its Start, and new Days count from its Start,** or back from its Due if that's all it has, as decision 191 has it on a task.
  - **In a pane under 420 px, each lane goes under its name.** The page doesn't redraw on crossing that width, only when it next draws.

## Releases 5.58 to 5.61: the rest of the visual review but its planning views (2026-11-02)

The owner asked for the review's next stages, and took its decisions V2, V3, V4, V6 and V7 as recommended. Stage V5 (the Timeline's one-row bar and Capacity's cells) stays with thread D's shared axis, as the review says, and V8 (dark theme) is still open. They're four stages built together in one worktree, as releases 5.58 to 5.61. No schema change and no new package.

**5.58, nothing cut off (stage V2):**
- **The window can be half of a 1920 px screen.** It was at least 1157 px, because a `QStackedWidget` holds its largest page's minimum, so the Priorities board set every view's least width. `ViewStack` asks only for the page on show. The sidebar keeps 248 px, so its names aren't cut, and the pane on the right has at least 340 px. The widest view now needs 942 px. Priorities' board scrolls across, each column at least 216 px, so five columns show at 1440 px.
- **A narrow list drops columns** (`RowTree._fit_columns`): the one after the title goes first, since a date at the row's end says more than a state, until the title has 160 px.
- **The task page fits its pane.** Its Notes heading held it at 481 px in a 288 px pane, so words ran off the edge. The format bar takes a line of its own when it can't fit (`TaskPane._fit_head`).
- **Words end in "…"** with all of them in the tooltip: the meeting's agenda heading and items, and a Priorities card's path (`ElidedLabel`, `ElidedCheck`, which paint the shorter text, so `text()` stays whole).
- **The Timeline's axis:** a date the Today tag would cover is left out, the tag has room for its word, hours beside a bar move off the today line, and over a year the year goes with its first month and each January. Legend entries stay whole when it wraps.
- **Capacity:** the weeks share the width, the table shows all its rows when there's room, and there's no stray square where two scroll bars meet. Timeline's and Capacity's margins match the lists'.

**5.59, quieter windows (stage V3):**
- **A row's actions** lie over the end of its first column, on hover and on the current row (`ActionStrip`), and they're on a new right-click menu. A permanent column of links took up to 220 px of the title's room. Titles at 1440 px have nearly twice the width.
- **Collapse a level and Expand a level** are two icon buttons (two new icons), gone where nothing nests. A hidden control takes no room in a `FlowLayout` now.
- **Instructions wait for Show hints:** Timeline's and Priorities' placeholder cards, Plan mode's sentence, Priorities' digits and "Sorted for you", the Task view's footer and "Press P" (decision V7), Unfiled's box (a chip otherwise), Capacity's "Click a cell" and the mods log's keys. Keys still work, and F1, tooltips and Alt still name them.
- **One accent button** (decision V2): New capture. File to task, Save settings, Open task and the rest are neutral, where they were. What Enter does is unchanged.
- **One arrow family:** `DropButton` (its words, then a chevron from the icon set), `icons.set_fold`, `chev:` pictures on pages and a stroked chevron on the Timeline's folds. Menu buttons' chevrons sit close after their words, and capture's More has no "…" as well.
- **Windows' chrome:** `look.dress_window`, from the application's existing event filter, asks DWM for small round corners on popups and a base-colour title bar, so there's no seam. Windows 10 refuses, harmlessly. The palette has an edge, the sidebar button's state is in its icon, Select mode's ticks match the rest, and an error stays in the status line until the next message.

**5.60, one meaning per colour (stage V4):**
- **Every colour is a token.** `tests/test_visual_stages.py` fails on a raw colour in `docrepo/ui` outside `tokens.py` and `style.py`, or a line naming the Classic look, and Classic's sheets moved to `style.py`. `{C.text2}` names one in an f-string. The Timeline's retired greys and second red are gone, in both looks.
- **Selection keeps its blue to itself.** Chips, a mention, the drop overlay and information boxes use `info_bg`, and Capacity's 80 to 100% cells use caution's tint, changing decision 18, since a selected cell couldn't be told from a busy one. The clicked cell stays marked after a redraw.
- **State in a row** is words, with a chip only for Parked (decision V6). Note, Key date and Reminder in that column are words too. Activity's chips are all neutral, and its dates one line.
- **The Timeline** (decisions V3 and V4): plain names beside the colour edge, and lanes as one neutral band with a small coloured dot and dark words, which also ends the two names that failed contrast. The plant events lane goes when there's nothing in view, and its words are text with the flag in colour.
- **Parse and populate:** the marker is a small dot rather than a wand. With more than two fields filled each has only the dot (`CaptureWindow._settle_filled`), and a date's dot is at its left, clear of the calendar.

**5.61, forms and small things (stage V6):**
- **Edit task fits at 608 by 724 without scrolling.** It scrolled by 433 px on this computer. Hours, labels, Runs through weekends, reminder, recurrence, key dates and parents are under More options, which names them, and opens when something rarer is set, or for a task made from a template, whose hours and labels carry its marks. A checklist with lines stays in front. The window grows when it opens.
- **New task and Update don't scroll:** Labels and two pairs, the rest under More fields. Parse leaves it shut, as the Understood line names what it filled, in the new look.
- **Questions** (`widgets.ask`) name their answers, have no icon, and put Enter and Esc on the safe one.
- **Also:** a form's labels have one role; Settings' More hotkeys is a fold with a row each; Quick edit's controls are 28 px; chips have one 4 px corner in widgets; the calendar's weekends are grey, with chevrons; Import opens small; a new data root's Task tree and Capacity say what to do; and a list with no rows to pick has no headings, nor has Search's one column.

**Tests:** `tests/test_visual_stages.py` is new, and `tests/test_real_look.py` gains the window's least width on every view, the task page's width and the forms' scrolling, on Windows' real platform. Tests that clicked a row's link now go through `RowTree.action_strip`, as a person goes through the mouse. All tests pass, on top of releases 5.53 to 5.56: 2226, with 1 skipped. Two runs along the way had a `PermissionError` from Windows on replacing a temporary file in `SafeFS.write_text`, each in a different Today panel test. Each passed when run again, and the last full run was clean, so it comes and goes. The cause isn't known, though another program briefly holding the file is likely. (Release 5.40.1 found it: Qt's file watcher, on the wheels' config.)

**Hand-tested by the owner, merged to `main` and on GitHub** (2026-11-02). **Not checked yet:** the corporate computer. DWM's corners and title bar colour show only on a real window of Windows 11, so the corporate test asks about them too.

**Left for later,** beyond stage V5 and V8: command bars that overflow into More by width; the 41 px row where "deadline" adds a line; Documents' parent rows' cells; the empty status line's height; Settings' Data root row and a toggle for Show hints; the mods log's footer; a footer band on dialogs; date fields' placeholder and Update's State label; the palette's repeated words; the wheels' rings; the format bar on use. Bars' default colour and hatching are done in release 5.40.

## Decisions (releases 5.58 to 5.61)

- **The review's V2, V3, V4, V6 and V7:** as recommended, by the owner (2026-11-02).
- **Row actions stay words, not the review's 24 px icons.** Labels on frequent commands are what the playbook's evidence favours, and no icon says Discussed. They lie over the title's column rather than taking one, so a row's State and Due stay in view.
- **Pages' chips stay square-cornered.** Qt's rich text can't round them, and painting them as pictures would lose copying and Find. Chips in widgets take a small 4 px corner, so the two are close, rather than pills beside squares.
- **Decision 18 changed:** Capacity's middle band is caution's tint, not the accent's.
- **Priorities' columns are at least 216 px,** not the review's 260 to 300, so all five show at 1440 px. They reach 258 px at the 82% window on a 1920 px screen.



## Releases 5.97 to 5.100: consolidation (2026-11-06)

The design workspace's consolidation plan (`docs/mods/consolidation-plan.md`, items 111 to 116, stage 34, decisions 386 to 397), from the whole-app review of 2026-11-06 (its F4 and agreed action 13), taken as recommended by the owner on 2026-11-06. Fewer places, not more features: one daily list, one home for a task, one way per job. The plan asked for a clickable mock first; one was built the same night in the browse tool mockup, alongside this build, for the owner to review with it. No schema change, nothing removed from the data and no new package. Each release is its own row above; one corporate test covers all four, the design workspace's `handoff/docrepo/release-5-97-to-5-100-test.md`.

## Release 5.97: the Urgent view retired (2026-11-06)

The consolidation plan's items 111 and 116.

- **No Urgent view** (decision 386; `views.VIEW_LISTS`, `views.RETIRED_VIEWS`): the sidebar's Lists are Overdue and next 7 days, Reminders, Waiting on others, Assigned to me and No dates or hours. Urgent stays a list (`lists.LISTS`, so the troubleshooting report and the test vectors are as they were) and a tick on a task.
- **The task tree's Urgent only** (`TreeFilter.urgent`): the tasks the Urgent view listed, with their parents dimmed, as the first of the tree's filters, before Show parked; ticking it opens the branches down to each urgent task. With other filters on, a task matches all of them.
- **Today's Due in the next 7 days puts urgent tasks first** (`views.today_due_entries`), with their key dates, then the rest soonest first, each urgent task with its Urgent chip. The Today side panel's Due section has the same order (decision 388: it reads the same entries).
- **Its name still leads there:** `set_view("urgent")` and `show_browse("urgent")` open the task tree with Urgent only ticked, so a wheel's `browse.view` slot with `"view": "urgent"` still loads and works (the registry allows it), and the palette has **Urgent tasks (the task tree, Urgent only)** in the Urgent view's place.
- **The glossary** (decision 397): Priority in the decision's words, Urgent (a tick, the tree's filter, first in Today's Due), and Set aside moved to the retired words. Tagged label is there since 5.79, and What-if before it. The help for Urgent says where urgent tasks are now; README's "Blank capture" line no longer says set aside.

**Tests:** `tests/test_consolidation.py` (new): the sidebar, Urgent only and its branches, the old view's name from a wheel, a link and the palette, and Today's order and chip; `tests/test_views.py`: the views and lists, the filter, Today's order. Changed for the view's going: `test_nav_pane`, `test_today_panel`.

**Built in the `consolidation` worktree** (2026-11-06, overnight), from `main` at `cd75434` (release 5.101), with 5.97 to 5.100, merged to `main` as `d1f9bdc` and on GitHub 2026-11-07, without a hand test (the owner's choice), with their picks between the mock and the build.

## Release 5.98: Find files, and one Edit and one Add to (2026-11-06)

The consolidation plan's items 112 and 113.

- **Find files** (decisions 389 to 391; `docrepo/ui/task_view.py`, its module and class names kept): the Task view, renamed. Its title bar says "Find files: Installation". It opens from the task page's Files group, whose summary has **Find files** (and its body "All n in Find files"), from O, the palette's Find files and the Links group's All n links. The Files group shows with no files too ("none yet"), so a mouse way in is always there. The task page's bar has no Task view button.
- **Its bar has one button, Task page** (decision 390): Note, Update, Add to, Edit, New task under this one, Copy prompt and Open folder went from it, and so did its letters N, U, T, A, D, E and M (and Alt with them): typing in the list, letters too, goes to the search box; P still pins. Its palette keeps its own commands and Open task page. The header's "Add one" (an update) and the empty list's "Add a note with files" went with them; dropping files on the window still makes a note.
- **No Notes page tab** (decision 391): the kinds are All, Files, Emails, Notes and updates, and Subtasks (Ctrl+1 to 5). Enter on a note opens its block on its task page in the browse tool. The tab's code went (`TaskPane.notes_only` too); the browse window's notes pages are the task page's alone.
- **One Edit and one Add to** (decision 392; `docrepo/task_commands.py` without Qt, `docrepo/ui/task_menus.py` for the menus): Edit is always the same six, in the same order, each greyed with its reason when it doesn't apply: Edit task… (E), Move…, Put under…, Also put under…, Remove from under… and Discard… (with several parents, Move… and Remove from under… list them); Add to is Today (D) or On Today ✓, the open meetings, and An agenda… (A). On the task page's bar Edit is a split button: its words open Edit task (E), its chevron ("More ways to edit") the menu. Move…, Put under… and Also put under… pick from a list without the task and every task under it (5.103's `keep`, wired in when the two were merged). The task page's bar, every task row's right-click menu in the browse window (after its own actions, before State; a row's own Edit action is left to the Edit menu) and Find files' task rows make them from the same lists.
- **Old names in `wheels.jsonc` keep working** (`wheels_config.RENAMED`, `Arg.renamed`): `task_view.current` runs `find_files.current`, and `search.tasks`' `"then": "task_view"` is `find_files`; the schema accepts both, the file DocRepo writes for a new data root says the new ones, and `usage` counts the old rows under the new name. Task search's hint says Find files.

**Tests:** `tests/test_task_commands.py` (new, no Qt): the Edit anatomy for a subtask, a top-level task and two parents, and Add to. `tests/test_consolidation.py`: the bar's menus and the row menus against the shared lists, a Today row's Edit, the Files group and Find files' title, bar, keys and rows. `tests/test_task_view_notes.py` rewritten for Find files without its tab (a note opens on the task page; a save there reaches Find files). `test_wheels_config` (the old names loading) and `test_usage` (counted under the new). Changed for the new words and places: `test_detail`, `test_browse_window`, `test_open_links`, `test_selection_search`, `test_wheel_window`, `test_task_view`, `test_state_menu` (a row's menu: Add to, Edit, then State).

The consolidation plan's item 115.

The mode is kept in its draft (one from before this release, which kept none, comes back as it would have saved).

Make it a task, on a note on Today, stays.
